Book 4, Chapter 1

०१ वंश-विस्तार-कथनं, ब्रह्मणो दक्षादीनां चोत्पत्तिः, पुरूरवसो जन्म, रेवत्या सह बलरामस्य विवाहश् च

VP 4.1.1

भगवन्यन्नरैः कार्यं साधुकर्मण्यवस्थितैः । तन्मह्यं गुरुणाख्यातं नित्यनैमित्तिकात्मकम्

Maitreyar – Those who are established in saadhu karma, vaidika karmaas, whatever one has to do, that you told me in great detail. Nitya and Naimittika karmaas which are very much required, by those who are following the vaidika dharmaas, you told me about them.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhgvnynnraiḥ — Maitreyar – Those who are established in; kāryṃ — saadhu karma, vaidika karmaas, whatever one has to; sādhukrmṇyvsthitaiḥ — do, that you told me in great; tnmhyṃ — detail. Nitya and Naimittika karmaas which are very; guruṇākhyātṃ — much required, by those who are following; nitynaimittikātmkm — the vaidika dharmaas, you told me about them.

Śrīdhara

dhrmopdhrmyoḥ — Maitreyar –; prvm — Those who; uktyor — are established; anuvrṇyte — in saadhu karma,; mnu-vṃśś — vaidika karmaas,; cturthāṃśe — whatever one; prvrtk-nivrtkḥ — has to; nediṣṭh-vṃśṃ — do, that you; prthme'kthyd — told me; ytr — in great; raivtḥ — detail. Nitya; raivtīṃ — and Naimittika karmaas; hline — which are; prādāt — very much; sv-sutāṃ — required, by; druhiṇājñyā — those who are; uktānuvād-pūrvkṃ — following the; mnu-vṃśṃ — vaidika dharmaas,; pṛcchtibhgvn — you told; iti — me about them.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhgvnynnraiḥ kāryṃ sādhukrmṇyvsthitaiḥ | tnmhyṃ guruṇākhyātṃ nitynaimittikātmkm ||1||

Śrīdhara

dhrmopdhrmyoḥ prvm uktyor anuvrṇyte | mnu-vṃśś cturthāṃśe prvrtk-nivrtkḥ || nediṣṭh-vṃśṃ prthme'kthyd ytr raivtḥ | raivtīṃ hline prādāt sv-sutāṃ druhiṇājñyā || uktānuvād-pūrvkṃ mnu-vṃśṃ pṛcchti—bhgvn iti |

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

धर्मोपधर्मयोः पर्वम् उक्तयोर् अनुवर्ण्यते । मनु-वंशश् चतुर्थांशे प्रवर्तक-निवर्तकः ॥ नेदिष्ठ-वंशं प्रथमेऽकथयद् यत्र रैवतः । रैवतीं हलिने प्रादात् स्व-सुतां द्रुहिणाज्ञया ॥ उक्तानुवाद-पूर्वकं मनु-वंशं पृच्छति—भगवन् इति ।

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 1).

Maitreyar – Those who are established in saadhu karma, vaidika karmaas, whatever one has to do, that you told me in great detail. Nitya and Naimittika karmaas which are very much required, by those who are following the vaidika dharmaas, you told me about them.

VP 4.1.2

वर्णधर्मास्तथाख्याता धर्मा ये चाश्रमेषु च । श्रोतुमिच्छाम्यहं वंशं राज्ञां तद् ब्रूहि मे गुरो

Also the varna dharmaas were told - aashrama dharmaas were also told in detail by you. I am interested in listening to the vamsha of all the kings, who established all these things.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vrṇdhrmāstthākhyātā — Also the; dhrmā — varna dharmaas were; ye — told - aashrama; cāśrmeṣu — dharmaas were also; c — told in; śrotumicchāmyhṃ — detail by you.; vṃśṃ — I am interested; rājñāṃ — in listening to; td — the vamsha; brūhi — of all the; me — kings, who established; guro — all these things.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vrṇdhrmāstthākhyātā dhrmā ye cāśrmeṣu c | śrotumicchāmyhṃ vṃśṃ rājñāṃ td brūhi me guro ||2||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 4.1.3

मैत्रेय श्रूयतामयमनेकयज्वशूरवीरधीरभूपालालंकृतो ब्रह्मादिर्मानवो वंशः

Sri Paraasharar - Manu vamsha, I am going to tell starting from Chaturmukha Brahma, to all the Manus, it is decorated by such great kings, who were performing a lot of yaagaas, who were very valiant, and powerful.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

maitrey — Sri Paraasharar - Manu vamsha, I am going to; śrūytāmymnekyjvśūrvīrdhīrbhūpālālṃkṛto — tell starting from Chaturmukha Brahma, to all the Manus, it; brhmādirmānvo — is decorated by such great kings, who were performing; vṃśḥ — a lot of yaagaas, who were very valiant, and powerful.

Śrīdhara

vīr — Sri Paraasharar; utsāhvān — - Manu; śūrḥ — vamsha, I; prābhibhāvī — am going; brhmā — to tell starting; ādirmlkārṇṃ — from Chaturmukha; ysy — Brahma, to; sḥ — all the; mānvo — Manus, it is; vaivsvtsy — decorated by; mnoḥ — such great; smbndhī — kings, who; ttr — were performing a; prthmṃ — lot of; vṃśsmṇādiphlmāhbrhmādym — yaagaas, who; iti — were very; 3 — valiant, and powerful.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

maitrey śrūytāmymnekyjvśūrvīrdhīrbhūpālālṃkṛto brhmādirmānvo vṃśḥ ||3||

Śrīdhara

vīr utsāhvān, śūrḥ prābhibhāvī | brhmā ādirmlkārṇṃ ysy sḥ | mānvo vaivsvtsy mnoḥ smbndhī | ttr prthmṃ vṃśsmṇādiphlmāh—brhmādym iti || 3 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

वीर उत्साहवान्, शूरः पराभिभावी । ब्रह्मा आदिर्मलकारणं यस्य सः । मानवो वैवस्वतस्य मनोः सम्बन्धी । तत्र प्रथमं वंशस्मणादिफलमाह—ब्रह्माद्यम् इति ॥३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 3).

Sri Paraasharar - Manu vamsha, I am going to tell starting from Chaturmukha Brahma, to all the Manus, it is decorated by such great kings, who were performing a lot of yaagaas, who were very valiant, and powerful.

VP 4.1.4

तदस्य वंशस्यानुपूर्वीमशेषवंशपापप्रणाशनाय मैत्रेयैतां कथां शृणु

of 249 www.sadagopan.org

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tdsy — of; vṃśsyānupūrvīmśeṣvṃśpāpprṇāśnāy — of; maitreyaitāṃ — 249; kthāṃ — 249; śṛṇu — www.sadagopan.org

Śrīdhara

vīr — What; utsāhvān — is the; śūrḥ — purpose of; prābhibhāvī — this,; brhmā — all your; ādirmlkārṇṃ — sins will; ysy — get; sḥ — destroyed because; mānvo — it is; vaivsvtsy — such a; mnoḥ — dhaarmika; smbndhī — vamsha. Even; ttr — listening to; prthmṃ — it; vṃśsmṇādiphlmāhbrhmādym — will wash; iti — away all; 4 — our sins.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tdsy vṃśsyānupūrvīmśeṣvṃśpāpprṇāśnāy maitreyaitāṃ kthāṃ śṛṇu ||4||

Śrīdhara

vīr utsāhvān, śūrḥ prābhibhāvī | brhmā ādirmlkārṇṃ ysy sḥ | mānvo vaivsvtsy mnoḥ smbndhī | ttr prthmṃ vṃśsmṇādiphlmāh—brhmādym iti || 4 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

वीर उत्साहवान्, शूरः पराभिभावी । ब्रह्मा आदिर्मलकारणं यस्य सः । मानवो वैवस्वतस्य मनोः सम्बन्धी । तत्र प्रथमं वंशस्मणादिफलमाह—ब्रह्माद्यम् इति ॥४॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 4).

What is the purpose of this, all your sins will get destroyed because it is such a dhaarmika vamsha. Even listening to it will wash away all our sins.

VP 4.1.5

तद्यथा सकलजगतामादिरनादिभूतस्स ऋग्यजुस्सामादिमयो भगवान‍् विष्णुस्तस्य ब्रह्मणो मूर्त्तं रूपं हिरण्यगर्भो ब्रह्माण्डभूतो ब्रह्मा भगवान‍् प्राग्बभूव

Bhagavaan Vishnu is the sole creator of this whole world. He is beginningless. He is Ringmaya, Yajurmaya, Saamaveda maya, Bhagavaan Vishnu. He is praised by the Vedaas, He is established in the Vedaas. Every word of the Vedaas is telling about Him only. He is that Brahmaa's moorta svaroopa, that which is manifested. He is called Hiranyagarbha, and is the whole Brahmaanda itself. He is Chaturmukha Brahma who was born first. He is the first chetana in the Brahmaanda, called Hiranyagarbha.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tdythā — Bhagavaan Vishnu is the sole creator; skljgtāmādirnādibhūtss — of this whole world. He is; ṛgyjussāmādimyo — beginningless. He is Ringmaya, Yajurmaya, Saamaveda; bhgvān — maya, Bhagavaan Vishnu. He is praised; viṣṇustsy — by the Vedaas, He is established in; brhmṇo — the Vedaas. Every word of the; mūrttṃ — Vedaas is telling about Him only.; rūpṃ — He is that Brahmaa's moorta svaroopa,; hirṇygrbho — that which is manifested. He is called; brhmāṇḍbhūto — Hiranyagarbha, and is the whole Brahmaanda; brhmā — itself. He is Chaturmukha Brahma who; bhgvān — was born first. He is the; prāgbbhūv — first chetana in the Brahmaanda, called Hiranyagarbha.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tdythā skljgtāmādirnādibhūtss ṛgyjussāmādimyo bhgvān viṣṇustsy brhmṇo mūrttṃ rūpṃ hirṇygrbho brhmāṇḍbhūto brhmā bhgvān prāgbbhūv ||5||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 4.1.6

ब्रह्मणश्च दक्षिणाङ्गुष्ठजन्मा दक्षप्रजापतिः दक्षस्याप्यदितिरदितेर्विवस्वान‍् विवस्वतो मनुः

From Chaturmukha Brahma's right thumb, Daksha Prajaapati was born. From Daksha, Aditi was born. From Aditi, Vivasvaan, and from Vivasvaan, Manu was born.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

brhmṇśc — From Chaturmukha Brahma's; dkṣiṇāṅguṣṭhjnmā — right thumb, Daksha Prajaapati; dkṣprjāptiḥ — was born. From Daksha,; dkṣsyāpyditirditervivsvān — Aditi was born. From; vivsvto — Aditi, Vivasvaan, and from; mnuḥ — Vivasvaan, Manu was born.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

brhmṇśc dkṣiṇāṅguṣṭhjnmā dkṣprjāptiḥ dkṣsyāpyditirditervivsvān vivsvto mnuḥ ||6||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 4.1.7

मनोरिक्ष्वाकुनृगधृष्टशर्यातिनरिष्यन्तप्रांशुनाभागदिष्टकरूषपृषध्राख्या दश पुत्रा बभूवुः

Manu had nine sons. They are Iskhvaaku, Nruga, Drushta, Sharyaati, Narishyanta, Naabhaaga, Dishta, Karoosha and Prushadhra.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

mnorikṣvākunṛgdhṛṣṭśryātinriṣyntprāṃśunābhāgdiṣṭkrūṣpṛṣdhrākhyā — Manu had nine sons.; dś — They are Iskhvaaku, Nruga,; putrā — Drushta, Sharyaati, Narishyanta, Naabhaaga,; bbhūvuḥ — Dishta, Karoosha and Prushadhra.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

mnorikṣvākunṛgdhṛṣṭśryātinriṣyntprāṃśunābhāgdiṣṭkrūṣpṛṣdhrākhyā dś putrā bbhūvuḥ ||7||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 4.1.8

इष्टिं च मित्रावरुणयोर्मनुः पुत्रकामश्चकार

With the desire to beget sons, he performed yaaga Ishti for Mitra and Varuna.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

iṣṭiṃ — With the desire; c — to beget sons, he; mitrāvruṇyormnuḥ — performed yaaga Ishti; putrkāmśckār — for Mitra and Varuna.

Śrīdhara

ttr — Manu performed the; tāvt — Ishti first, and; aditir — then got all the; hy — sons. Ilaa was; ajniṣṭ — born first, and then; jniṣṭ — the nine sons; dkṣsy — were born. This is; duhitā — the order which; tāṃ — we have to; devā — understand, though in the; anujāynt — paatha krama, it; ity-ādi-śruti-siddhāṃ — is different. The Vaayu; vṃśānupūrvīm — Puraana pramaana is; āh — being told. ## 9

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

iṣṭiṃ c mitrāvruṇyormnuḥ putrkāmśckār ||8||

Śrīdhara

ttr tāvt aditir hy ajniṣṭ jniṣṭ dkṣsy duhitā, tāṃ devā anujāynt ity-ādi-śruti-siddhāṃ vṃśānupūrvīm āh—

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तत्र तावत् अदितिर् ह्य् अजनिष्ट जनिष्ट दक्षस्य दुहिता, तां देवा अनुजायन्त इत्य्-आदि-श्रुति-सिद्धां वंशानुपूर्वीम् आह—

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 8).

Manu performed the Ishti first, and then got all the sons. Ilaa was born first, and then the nine sons were born. This is the order which we have to understand, though in the paatha krama, it is different. The Vaayu Puraana pramaana is being told. ## 9

VP 4.1.9

तत्र तावदपह्नुते होतुरपचारादिला नाम कन्या बभूव

He did Putrakaameshti for Mitra, Varuna; there was a mistake by the hotru during the yaaga. Because of the mistake they did, instead of of 249 www.sadagopan.org getting a son, he got a daughter, by name Ilaa. After that, the sons were born.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ttr — He did Putrakaameshti for Mitra, Varuna; there; tāvdphnute — was a mistake by the hotru during; hoturpcārādilā — the yaaga. Because of the mistake they; nām — did, instead of of 249 www.sadagopan.org getting; knyā — a son, he got a daughter, by; bbhūv — name Ilaa. After that, the sons were born.

Śrīdhara

sudyumnsy — Śrīdhara explains the; prthmṃ — preceding passage in; knyātvād — relation to the; rājyānrhtvṃ — chapter’s teaching; the; drśynn — exact Sanskrit note; āh — is preserved above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ttr tāvdphnute hoturpcārādilā nām knyā bbhūv ||9||

Śrīdhara

sudyumnsy prthmṃ knyātvād rājyānrhtvṃ drśynn āh

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

सुद्युम्नस्य प्रथमं कन्यात्वाद् राज्यानर्हत्वं दर्शयन्न् आह

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 9).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.1.10

सैवच मित्रावरुणयोः प्रसादात्सुद्युम्नो नाम मनोः पुत्रो मैत्रेय आसीत्

Then he worshipped Mitra and Varuna, and with their prasaada, Ilaa who was born as a daughter, she got turned into a male by name Sudyumna.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

saivc — Then he worshipped; mitrāvruṇyoḥ — Mitra and Varuna,; prsādātsudyumno — and with their; nām — prasaada, Ilaa who was; mnoḥ — born as a; putro — daughter, she got; maitrey — turned into a; āsīt — male by name Sudyumna.

Transliteration

saivc mitrāvruṇyoḥ prsādātsudyumno nām mnoḥ putro maitrey āsīt ||10||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 4.1.11

पुनश्चेश्वरकोपात्स्त्री सती सा तु सोमसूनोः बुधस्याश्रमसमीपे बभ्राम

Again that Sudyumna was roaming around in Kailaasa where Eeshvara was in ekaanta; there is a saying that whoever comes when they are in ekaanta will turn into a woman. So, he again turned into a woman. Near the Budha's aashrama, he was roaming around, after becoming a woman.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

punśceśvrkopātstrī — Again that Sudyumna was roaming around in; stī — Kailaasa where Eeshvara was in ekaanta; there; sā — is a saying that whoever comes when; tu — they are in ekaanta will turn into; somsūnoḥ — a woman. So, he again turned into; budhsyāśrmsmīpe — a woman. Near the Budha's aashrama, he; bbhrām — was roaming around, after becoming a woman.

Śrīdhara

īśvrkopād — Again that; itimhādevḥ — Sudyumna was; kil — roaming around; ilāvṛte — in Kailaasa; pārvtyā — where Eeshvara; sh — was in; rmmāṇḥ — ekaanta; there; kencin — is a saying; nimittenāśptyo'smin — that whoever; prvekṣyti2 — comes when; s — they are; yoṣid — in ekaanta; bhviṣyti — will turn; iti — into a; sudyumns — woman. So,; tṃ — he again turned; śāpm — into a; ajānn — woman. Near; ttr — the Budha's; prviṣṭo — aashrama, he; yoṣid — was roaming; bbhūveti — around, after; 11 — becoming a woman.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

punśceśvrkopātstrī stī sā tu somsūnoḥ budhsyāśrmsmīpe bbhrām ||11||

Śrīdhara

īśvrkopād iti—mhādevḥ kil ilāvṛte pārvtyā sh rmmāṇḥ kencin nimittenāśpt—yo'smin prvekṣyti2, s yoṣid bhviṣyti iti | sudyumns tṃ śāpm ajānn ttr prviṣṭo yoṣid bbhūveti || 11 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

ईश्वरकोपाद् इति—महादेवः किल इलावृते पार्वत्या सह रममाणः केनचिन् निमित्तेनाशपत्—योऽस्मिन् प्रवेक्ष्यति२, स योषिद् भविष्यति इति । सुद्युम्नस् तं शापम् अजानन् तत्र प्रविष्टो योषिद् बभूवेति ॥११॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 11).

Again that Sudyumna was roaming around in Kailaasa where Eeshvara was in ekaanta; there is a saying that whoever comes when they are in ekaanta will turn into a woman. So, he again turned into a woman. Near the Budha's aashrama, he was roaming around, after becoming a woman.

VP 4.1.13

जातेऽपि तस्मिन्नमिततेजोभिः परमर्षिभिरिष्टिमय ऋङ्मयो यजुर्मयस्साममयोऽथर्वणमयस्सर्ववेदमयो मनोमयो ज्ञानमयो न किञ्चिन्मयोऽन्नमयो भगवान‍् यज्ञपुरुषस्वरूपी सुद्युम्नस्य पुंस्त्वमभिलषद्भिर्यथावदिष्टस्तत्प्रसादादिला पुनरपि सुद्युम्नोऽभवत्

Due to Eeshvara's shaapa, Sudyumna had again turned into Ilaa. After that, Yajnyapurusha, Mahaavishnu was again worshipped, with the ishti, with the desire to make Ilaa again into a man. The attributes of the Yajnyapurusha are told here, as Rungmaya, Yajurmaya, Saamamaya. He is the one praised by all the Vedaas. All the Vedic shabdaas are telling Paramaatman only. This is told also as Atharvanamaya, Sarvamaya, Manomaya (parishuddha mano graahyah), Jnyaanamaya, Vijnyaanamaya, Annamaya, Amrutamaya. Na kinchit maya means that He did not have any jnyaana sankocha like deva and others, who were all karma vashyaas, kshetrajnyaas only. Because He is the Supreme yajnya purusha Bhagavaan. The rishis desired and performed the ishti. They wanted Ilaa to become a man, desiring the manhood of Sudyumna. With His grace, Ilaa again became Sudyumna.

All the sons, those related to him became kshatriyaas, and were present in all directions. Prushadhra, son of Manu, one of the nine sons of Manu; he killed a cow belonging to his guru, and because of that, he became a shoodra.

VP 4.1.18

tsy c putrotptteruttrkālmev vaiśytvprāptyā tt putrsy bhlndnsy kṣtriytvm aviruddhmev | at ev tdnvysy mruttsy ckrvrttitvṃ sṅgcchte | avikṣer ev kvcidvikṣiditypi nām || 31 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तस्य च पुत्रोत्पत्तेरुत्तरकालमेव वैश्यत्वप्राप्तया तत् पुत्रस्य भलन्दनस्य क्षत्रियत्वम् अविरुद्धमेव । अत एव तदन्वयस्य मरुत्तस्य चक्रवर्त्तित्वं सङ्गच्छते । अविक्षेर् एव क्वचिदविक्षिदित्यपि नाम ॥३१॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 31).

Avikshit had a powerful and valiant son by name Marutta. Marutta was very famous, and there are two shlokaas which are quite popular, even to this day.

VP 4.1.33

अमाद्यदिन्द्रस्सोमेन दक्षिणाभिर्द्विजातयः । मरुतः परिवेष्टारस्सदस्याश्च दिवौकसः

In that yajnya, after having received Somarasa, Indra felt great joy. All the Brahmins were very satisfied with the dakshinaa they received. They were all feeding. All the persons sitting there in the sadas were all gods, devataas.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

amādydindrssomen — In that yajnya, after having received Somarasa,; dkṣiṇābhirdvijātyḥ — Indra felt great joy. All the Brahmins were; mrutḥ — very satisfied with the dakshinaa they received.; priveṣṭārssdsyāśc — They were all feeding. All the persons sitting; divauksḥ — there in the sadas were all gods, devataas.

Śrīdhara

amādyt — In that yajnya,; sompānenātitṛptyā — after having received Somarasa,; hṛṣṭo — Indra felt great joy.; bbhūvety — All the Brahmins were; arthḥ — very satisfied with the; mruto — dakshinaa they received.; devāḥ — They were all feeding.; priveṣṭārḥ — All the persons sitting; annādipriveśkāḥ — there in the sadas; 33 — were all gods, devataas.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

amādydindrssomen dkṣiṇābhirdvijātyḥ | mrutḥ priveṣṭārssdsyāśc divauksḥ ||33||

Śrīdhara

amādyt sompānenātitṛptyā hṛṣṭo bbhūvety arthḥ | mruto devāḥ | priveṣṭārḥ annādipriveśkāḥ || 33 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अमाद्यत् सोमपानेनातितृप्तया हृष्टो बभूवेत्य् अर्थः । मरुतो देवाः । परिवेष्टारः अन्नादिपरिवेशकाः ॥३३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 33).

In that yajnya, after having received Somarasa, Indra felt great joy. All the Brahmins were very satisfied with the dakshinaa they received. They were all feeding. All the persons sitting there in the sadas were all gods, devataas.

VP 4.1.69

तच्च त्रिमार्गपरिवृत्तैरनेकयुगपरिवृत्तिं तिष्ठन्नपि रैवतश्शृण्वन्मुहूर्त्तमिव मेने

These are special details about the music. There are three maargaas told - Chitra, Dakshina and Dhaatraa. Though many yugaas passed by as he was listening to that beautiful music, he thought he had just spent a muhoorta. A muhoorta is said to be 40 minutes. 30 muhoortaas is a day. It went by so fast that he did not even realize that so many yugaas went.

VP 4.1.83

न ह्यादिमध्यान्तमजस्य यस्य विद्मो वयं सर्वमयस्य धातुः । न च स्वरूपं न परं स्वभावं न चैव सारं परमेश्वरस्य

Brahma - He starts to describe the greatness and qualities of Parabrahma, and that one of His avataaras is going to manifest in the pruthivi, earth. For that, he starts to praise Bhagavaan. This is the Brahma Stuti of Paramaatman. There is no end, there is no beginning of that unborn one. We do not know His beginning or end. He is everything. He is the Creator of the world. He is everything, present everywhere. One cannot know His jnyaana aananda svaroopa.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

n — Brahma - He starts; hyādimdhyāntmjsy — to describe the greatness and; ysy — qualities of Parabrahma, and that; vidmo — one of His avataaras is; vyṃ — going to manifest in the; srvmysy — pruthivi, earth. For that,; dhātuḥ — he starts to praise Bhagavaan.; n — This is the Brahma Stuti; c — of Paramaatman. There is no; svrūpṃ — end, there is no beginning; n — of that unborn one. We; prṃ — do not know His; svbhāvṃ — beginning or end. He is; n — everything. He is the Creator; caiv — of the world. He is; sārṃ — everything, present everywhere. One cannot; prmeśvrsy — know His jnyaana aananda svaroopa.

Śrīdhara

pūrv — He; cintitebhyo'nyebhyo'pyymev — has; vrḥ — all; śreṣṭh — the; iti — jnyaana,; vdn — bala, which; bldevāy — are; knyāṃ — svaabhaavikee,; dehītyāhn — natural; hyādimdhyāntm — to; iti — Him,; dśbhiḥ — not given; pūrvycchbdānāṃ — by; s — anyone.; viṣṇuḥ — That; dhritryāṃ — Parameshvara's; svāśenāvtīrṇ — prabhaava,; ityṣṭmenānvyḥ — svaroopa, who; ādimdhyāntyjñānhetuḥ — can; kārṇtyā — know?; srvgtsy — How; svrūpṃ — He; tttvṃ — supports; prṃ — everything, controls; svbhāvm — everything,; asādhārṇṃ — that; dhrmsārṃ — power; blm — we; 83 — cannot know.

Transliteration

Śrīdhara

pūrv cintitebhyo'nyebhyo'pyymev vrḥ śreṣṭh iti vdn bldevāy knyāṃ dehītyāh—n hyādimdhyāntm iti dśbhiḥ pūrvycchbdānāṃ s viṣṇuḥ dhritryāṃ svāśenāvtīrṇ ityṣṭmenānvyḥ | ādimdhyāntyjñānhetuḥ kārṇtyā srvgtsy svrūpṃ tttvṃ prṃ svbhāvm asādhārṇṃ dhrmsārṃ blm || 83 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

पूर्व चिन्तितेभ्योऽन्येभ्योऽप्ययमेव वरः श्रेष्ठ इति वदन बलदेवाय कन्यां देहीत्याह—न ह्यादिमध्यान्तम् इति दशभिः पूर्वयच्छब्दानां स विष्णुः धरित्र्यां स्वाशेनावतीर्ण इत्यष्टमेनान्वयः । आदिमध्यान्त्यज्ञानहेतुः कारणतया सर्वगतस्य स्वरूपं तत्त्वं परं स्वभावम् असाधारणं धर्मसारं बलम् ॥८३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 83).

He has all the jnyaana, bala, which are svaabhaavikee, natural to Him, not given by anyone. That Parameshvara's prabhaava, svaroopa, who can know? How He supports everything, controls everything, that power we cannot know.

VP 4.1.84

कलामुहूर्त्तादिमयश्च कालो न यद्विभूतेः परिणामहेतुः । अजन्मनाशस्य सदैकमूर्त्ते- रनामरूपस्य सनातनस्य

His vibhooti, which is Paramapada. All the parinaama which happens here due to kaala - kalaa, muhoorta, kaastha, dina, paksha, ahoraatra, ayana, samvatsara, do not happen in His vibhooti, nitya vibhooti. The kaala which is having all these divisions, does not cause any change or of 249 www.sadagopan.org parinaama in the nitya vibhooti. He does not have naama, roopa, He can be present as anything, any name, any form, anywhere. He is everything, He is sanaatana. He is nitya. He does not have janma, naasha. He is unchanging.

Śrīdhara

ysy vibhūtrvātārrūpāyāḥ priṇāmsy sdbhāvvikārsy kālo n hetuḥ | atr hetuḥ ajnm iti || 84 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

यस्य विभूतरवाताररूपायाः परिणामस्य सद्भावविकारस्य कालो न हेतुः । अत्र हेतुः अजन्म इति ॥८४॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 84).

Paramapada is not under the control of kaala. There, kaala is under the control of Paramaatman. Nothing happens there under the control of kaala. It is apraakruta. Janma, naasha due to karma does not happen to Him. Because of karma, being born with a body is not there for Him. Association with the body is janma, and separation from the body is marana, because jeevaatman is also nitya - this is not there for Him. Due to karma, the baddhaas get the deva and other forms and names. This is not there for Paramaatman.

VP 4.1.85

यस्य प्रसादादहमच्युतस्य भूतः प्रजासृष्टिकरोऽन्तकारी । क्रोधाच्च रुद्रः स्थितिहेतुभूतो यस्माच्च मध्ये पुरुषः परस्मात्

I have become the creator because of the grace of whom - it is Paramaatman only. I am born from His prasaada, His pleasant mind, His sattva. Rudra is born from His krodha. He Himself becomes the sthiti hetu bhoota, in between Chaturmukha Brahma and Rudra. Vishnu is His own avataara, and He takes care of sustenance.

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 85).

He is born Himself, as Raama and other avataaraas, as Vishnu.

VP 4.1.86

VP 4.1.87

पाकाय योऽग्नित्वमुपैति लोका- न्बिभर्त्ति पृथ्वीवपुरव्ययात्मा । शक्रादिरूपी परिपाति विश्व- मर्केन्दुरूपश्च तमो हिनस्ति

In order that everything can be cooked by fire, He takes the form of Agni. He takes the form of Pruthvi, and supports all the worlds. He is unchanging. Everything being His shareera, He controls everything being the inner controller of everything. He nurtures the whole world taking the form of Indra and others. He takes the form of Sun and Moon, and destroys the darkness of this world.

He takes the form of Sun and Moon, and destroys the darkness of this world.

VP 4.1.89

यस्सृज्यते सर्गकृदात्मनैव यः पाल्यते पालयिता च देवः । विश्वात्मकस्संह्रियतेऽन्तकारी पृथक्त्रयस्यास्यचयोऽव्ययात्मा

The creator creates everything by Himself. He Himself became many. He of 249 www.sadagopan.org nurtures everything, and He Himself is the nurturer of all. He is the destroyer who withdraws everything unto Himself. All these three functions he does separately. He is unchanging, avikaari.

Śrīdhara

trhi kiṃ sṛjyādystto bhinnā ityāśṅky sṛṣṭdīnnūdy sṛjyādibhiḥ shābhedenāh—yḥ sṛjyt iti dvābhyām | viśvātmno viśvsy antkārī sṃhṛyte c | asy srṣṭrāditrysy c yḥ pṛthk śuddhcidrūpḥ pṛthg ysyeti pāṭhe ysmāt pṛthgnyo nāstītyrthḥ || 89 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तर्हि किं सृज्यादयस्ततो भिन्ना इत्याशङ्क्य सृष्टदीननूद्य सृज्यादिभिः सहाभेदेनाह—यः सृज्यत इति द्वाभ्याम् । विश्वात्मनो विश्वस्य अन्तकारी संहृयते च । अस्य स्रष्ट्रादित्रयस्य च यः पृथक् शुद्धचिद्रूपः पृथग् यस्येति पाठे यस्मात् पृथगन्यो नास्तीत्यर्थः ॥८९॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 89).

One who is the antaraatmaa of the entire world. He creates by Himself. There is another paatha which says that there is nothing which is separated from Him. Everything is Bhagavat aatmaka. There is nothing which abrahmaatmaka. Everything is Brahmaatmaka is the meaning. This is told in Bhagavad Gita as " Na tat asti vinaa yat syaat mayaa bhootam charaacharam".

VP 4.1.90

यस्मिञ्जगद्यो जगदेतदाद्यो यश्चाश्रितोऽस्मिञ्जगति स्वयम्भूः । ससर्वभूतप्रभवो धरित्र्यां स्वांशेन विष्णुर्नृपतेऽवतीर्णः

He Himself is the whole world, and everything is in Him. He is the one who is supporter of everything. Everything is standing on Him means that everything is in Him only. Everything is shareera to Him. The whole world is Him only. Jagat cha sah was told in the beginning. Everything is His prakaara, being His shareera. He only is present as everything. Shareera vaachaka shabdaas go upto shareeri. Names which are used to connote the body, extend up to the inner self. All the names extend up to Paramaatman in the aparyavasaana vrutti. He is the creator of everything. In this world, Svayambhu is also supported in Him only. He Himself is Svayambhu. He is the one who is creator of all beings. Through His own amsha, He has come down to this world, that Vishnu, He is manifesting. He has done His avataara, with His own amsha, Vishnu.

VP 4.1.92

तस्मै त्वमेनां तनयां नरेन्द्र प्रयच्छ मायामनुजाय जायाम् । श्लाघ्यो वरोऽसौ तनया तवेयं स्त्रीरत्नभूता सदृशो हि योगः

tsmai tvmenāṃ tnyāṃ nrendr prycch māyāmnujāy jāyām | ślāghyo vro'sau tnyā tveyṃ strīrtnbhūtā sdṛśo hi yogḥ ||92||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 4.1.93

इतीरितोऽसौ कमलोद्भवेन भुवं समासाद्य पतिः प्रजानाम् । ददर्श ह्रस्वान‍् पुरुषान् विरूपा- नल्पौजसस्स्वल्पविवेकवीर्यान्

Sri Paraasharar - After Chaturmukha Brahma tells him all these details, the king Raivata comes to the earth, and sees that everyone is so dwarfish, so small. Because so many chaturyugaas have gone. They were all viroopa. They had very little tejas. They had very little viveka, veerya. Compared to him, he sees all of them as very diminutive, and with very little knowledge.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

itīrito'sau — Sri Paraasharar - After Chaturmukha; kmlodbhven — Brahma tells him all these details,; bhuvṃ — the king Raivata comes to the; smāsādy — earth, and sees that everyone is; ptiḥ — so dwarfish, so small. Because so; prjānām — many chaturyugaas have gone. They; ddrś — were all viroopa. They had very; hrsvān — little tejas. They had very little; puruṣān — viveka, veerya. Compared to him, he; virūpā- — sees all of them as very; nlpaujsssvlpvivekvīryān — diminutive, and with very little knowledge.

Śrīdhara

alpaujso'lpsāmrthyān — Sri Paraasharar - After Chaturmukha Brahma tells him all these details, the king Raivata comes to the earth, and sees that everyone is so dwarfish, so small. Because so many chaturyugaas have; 93 — gone. They were all viroopa. They had very little tejas. They had very little viveka, veerya. Compared to him, he sees all of them as very diminutive, and with very little knowledge.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

itīrito'sau kmlodbhven bhuvṃ smāsādy ptiḥ prjānām | ddrś hrsvān puruṣān virūpā- nlpaujsssvlpvivekvīryān ||93||

Śrīdhara

alpaujso'lpsāmrthyān || 93 ||

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अल्पौजसोऽल्पसामर्थ्यान् ॥९३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 93).

Sri Paraasharar - After Chaturmukha Brahma tells him all these details, the king Raivata comes to the earth, and sees that everyone is so dwarfish, so small. Because so many chaturyugaas have gone. They were all viroopa. They had very little tejas. They had very little viveka, veerya. Compared to him, he sees all of them as very diminutive, and with very little knowledge.

VP 4.1.94

कुशस्थलीं तां च पुरीमुपेत्य दृष्ट्वान‍्यरूपां प्रददौ स कन्याम् । सीरायुधाय स्फटिकाचलाभ- वक्षःस्थलायातुलधीर्नरेन्द्रः

He comes to the city of Kushasthalee. It was looking very different from what it was when he used to be there. He gives the kanyaa to Seeraayudha, who is Balaraama. Whose vakshasthala had the hue of great sphatika parvata. of 249 www.sadagopan.org

He; s — gives the kanyaa to; knyām — Seeraayudha, who is Balaraama.; sīrāyudhāy — Whose vakshasthala had the; sphṭikāclābh- — hue of great sphatika; vkṣḥsthlāyātuldhīrnrendrḥ — parvata. of 249 www.sadagopan.org

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 94).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

VP 4.1.95

उच्चप्रमाणामिति तामवेक्ष्य स्वलाङ्गलाग्रेण च तालकेतुः । विनम्रयामास ततश्च सापि बभूव सद्यो वनिता यथान्या

Taalaketu, Balaraama saw that Revati was very tall, and big compared to him. Because all the people at that time, were very diminutive comparatively, as so many chaturyugaas had already passed. He took his halaayudha, and with its edge, he pulled her down, and immediately she became as diminutive as all other people, and suitable to him.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

uccprmāṇāmiti — Taalaketu, Balaraama saw that; tāmvekṣy — Revati was very tall, and; svlāṅglāgreṇ — big compared to him. Because; c — all the people at that; tālketuḥ — time, were very diminutive; vinmryāmās — comparatively, as so many chaturyugaas; ttśc — had already passed. He took; sāpi — his halaayudha, and with its; bbhūv — edge, he pulled her; sdyo — down, and immediately she became; vnitā — as diminutive as all other; ythānyā — people, and suitable to him.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

uccprmāṇāmiti tāmvekṣy svlāṅglāgreṇ c tālketuḥ | vinmryāmās ttśc sāpi bbhūv sdyo vnitā ythānyā ||95||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 4.1.96

तां रेवतीं रैवतभूपकन्यां सीरायुधोऽसौ विधिनोपयेमे । दत्त्वाथ कन्यां स नृपो जगाम हिमालयं वै तपसे धृतात्मा

Balaraama, according to vidhi, he married her, after making her short, suitable to him, as per people who were existing at that time. After having given his daughter in marriage to Balaraama, King Raivata went to Himaalaya for doing tapas. This is the story of Raivata and Revati. Raivata and Revati go to Chaturmukha Brahma, and get mesmerized by listening to the music of Gandharvaas there, spends many chaturyugaas there, and comes back and sees that nobody is there; Chaturmukha Brahma says that Krishna has manifested there, and His brother Balaraama is there, who is also His amsha manifested as a human, that He is the right person for his daughter. This is the story of marriage of Revati and Balaraama. This concludes Chapter 1. || Iti Sri Vishnu Puraane Chaturthe Amshe Prathamo Adhyaayah || || Iti Sri Vishnu Chitteeye Sri Vishnu Puraana Vyaakhyaane Chaturthe Amshe Prathamo Adhyaayah ||

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tāṃ — Balaraama, according to vidhi, he married her, after making her; revtīṃ — short, suitable to him, as per people who were existing at; raivtbhūpknyāṃ — that time. After having given his daughter in marriage to; sīrāyudho'sau — Balaraama, King Raivata went to Himaalaya for doing tapas. This is; vidhinopyeme — the story of Raivata and Revati. Raivata and Revati go to; dttvāth — Chaturmukha Brahma, and get mesmerized by listening to the music; knyāṃ — of Gandharvaas there, spends many chaturyugaas there, and comes back and; s — sees that nobody is there; Chaturmukha Brahma says that Krishna has; nṛpo — manifested there, and His brother Balaraama is there, who is; jgām — also His amsha manifested as a human, that He is the; himālyṃ — right person for his daughter. This is the story of marriage; vai — of Revati and Balaraama. This concludes Chapter 1. || Iti; tpse — Sri Vishnu Puraane Chaturthe Amshe Prathamo Adhyaayah || || Iti Sri; dhṛtātmā — Vishnu Chitteeye Sri Vishnu Puraana Vyaakhyaane Chaturthe Amshe Prathamo Adhyaayah ||

तमतीव महारौद्रं मृत्युवक्त्रमिवापरम् । विलोक्य चिन्तयामास भगवान‍्मधुसूदनः

Śrīdhara

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

mūrcchāmupāyyau bhrāntyā nāgḥ kṛṣṇsy reckaiḥ | dṇḍpātnipāten vvām rudhirṃ bhu ||46||

Śrīdhara

mūrcchām iti | kṛṣṇsy bhrāntyā nṛtyto bhrmeṇ mūrcchāṃ nāgḥ prāptḥ | dṇḍvt pāto dṇḍ-pātḥ tīvr-prhārḥ tdvn nipāten reckair mukh-nāsikā-rndhrair bhu rudhirṃ vvām chrditvān | pāṭhāntre dṇḍvt-pād-nipāteneti s evārthḥ | yd vā, bhrānti-reck-dṇḍpātākhyāḥ nṛttoktāḥ pād-nyās-viśeṣāḥ | ythāh bhrtḥ— antr-bhrmrikā jñeyā bhrmrī bāhy-pūrvikā | algn-bhrmrī c syād ucit-bhrmrī tthā ॥ citr-bhrmrikā caiv ckr-bhrmrikā tthā | tiryg-bhrmrikā ceti bhrmāḥ spt prkīrtitāḥ | pārśvāt pārśvsy gmnṃ skhlitaiś clitaiḥ pdaiḥ | vividhaiś caiv pādaiś c pād-reck ucyte | [nā.śā. 4.249] nūpurṃ crṇṃ kṛtvā purtḥ smprsāryet | kṣiprm āviddh-krṇṃ dṇḍ-pāteti sā smṛtā ॥[nā.śā. 10.44] iti ॥46॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

मूर्च्छाम् इति । कृष्णस्य भ्रान्त्या नृत्यतो भ्रमेण मूर्च्छां नागः प्राप्तः । दण्डवत् पातो दण्ड-पातः तीव्र-प्रहारः तद्वन् निपातेन रेचकैर् मुख-नासिका-रन्ध्रैर् बहु रुधिरं ववाम छर्दितवान् । पाठान्तरे दण्डवत्-पाद-निपातेनेति स एवार्थः । यद् वा, भ्रान्ति-रेचक-दण्डपाताख्याः नृत्तोक्ताः पाद-न्यास-विशेषाः । यथाह भरतः— अन्तर्-भ्रमरिका ज्ञेया भ्रमरी बाह्य-पूर्विका । अलग्न-भ्रमरी च स्याद् उचित-भ्रमरी तथा ॥ चित्र-भ्रमरिका चैव चक्र-भ्रमरिका तथा । तिर्यग्-भ्रमरिका चेति भ्रमाः सप्त प्रकीर्तिताः । पार्श्वात् पार्श्वस्य गमनं स्खलितैश् चलितैः पदैः । विविधैश् चैव पादैश् च पाद-रेचक उच्यते । [ना.शा. ४.२४९] नूपुरं चरणं कृत्वा पुरतः सम्प्रसारयेत् । क्षिप्रम् आविद्ध-करणं दण्ड-पातेति सा स्मृता ॥[ना.शा. १०.४४] इति ॥४६॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 46).

Śrīdhara explains the preceding passage in relation to the chapter’s teaching; the exact Sanskrit note is preserved above.

ttheti — Śrīdhara; tvtto — Śrīdhara; dṇḍo'pi — explains; vrḥ — the; ślāghytvāt — the; tvd — preceding; anyto — passage; vro'pipi — passage; n — in; ślāghyḥ — relation; s — relation; soḍhvyo — to; myā — the; dṇḍs — the; tvtto — chapter’s; mām — teaching;; ety — teaching;; ayṃ — the; vrḥiti — exact; pāṭhe — exact; dṇḍ-miṣeṇāyṃ — Sanskrit; vrḥ — note; tvtto — note; mām — is; upaiti — preserved; duṣṭ-svbhāv-nivṛtteḥ — preserved; 75 — above.

ttheti | tvtto dṇḍo'pi vrḥ ślāghytvāt | tvd anyto vro'pipi n ślāghyḥ | "s soḍhvyo myā dṇḍs tvtto mām ety ayṃ vrḥ"iti pāṭhe dṇḍ-miṣeṇāyṃ vrḥ tvtto mām upaiti duṣṭ-svbhāv-nivṛtteḥ ॥75॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तथेति । त्वत्तो दण्डोऽपि वरः श्लाघ्यत्वात् । त्वद् अन्यतो वरोऽपिपि न श्लाघ्यः । "स सोढव्यो मया दण्डस् त्वत्तो माम् एत्य् अयं वरः"इति पाठे दण्ड-मिषेणायं वरः त्वत्तो माम् उपैति दुष्ट-स्वभाव-निवृत्तेः ॥७५॥
